HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_23a3d2ef0ed226239adb","text":"1. **Human speech as bewildered perception, not answer.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B001` uttered speech; `ء ن س B002` perceiving something into awareness by seeing/sensing/hearing. Structural cue: the human speaks only after earth-shaking and earth-output, before earth itself reports. Assigned role: the human’s saying is the first conscious registration of the event, but it has question-form rather than knowledge. Abductive move: I infer that the speech arises because perception has been forced by visible disturbance; the packet gives perception and utterance, I supply the cognitive lag. Reading change: `ما لها` reads less as a rhetorical phrase and more as human awareness catching up to a nonhuman disclosure already underway.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_241b7ecd86a6dff237ac","text":"The later `ليروا أعمالهم` corrected my first tendency to treat 99:3 as only panic: it is also a failed or premature epistemic act. Human `قول` asks “what is with her?” before earth’s `أخبار` and before compelled `ر ء ي`; later seeing replaces earlier supposing. The singular `الإنسان` also became more collective after 99:6’s `الناس أشتاتا`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001","root_001272/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_26bdb13afc14457c43df","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:3:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B001\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Soru, varsayıma dayalı ilk konuşma dönüşüdür; yerin görevlendirilmiş haberi ona bilgiye dayalı cevap verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4e77611b342be2cd1d92","text":"2. **The question marks a rupture of familiarity.** Fixed `ء ن س/B003` supplies familiar comfort that removes estrangement. Structurally, the ordinarily inhabited earth first convulses and expels contents, after which the human asks what is with it; the familiar ground has become an object of inquiry. The dormant familiarity branch assigns the human an affective relation to the earth, and `مَا لَهَا` marks that relation's failure. The assumption that prior earthly stability generated familiarity is inferred. The exclamation then reads as estrangement from what had been the human's most familiar material setting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B003","root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5e642e296140e302f99a","text":"3. **Human speech opens a commissioned question-and-testimony exchange.** Fixed `ق و ل/B001` supplies articulated utterance, while `B011` supplies saying with the force of supposition, especially in a question. In the next two ayat the earth gives report-like speech (`ح د ث/B003`) grounded in tested inner knowledge (`خ ب ر/B001`), and that speech is traced to hidden imparting (`و ح ي/B001`). The fixed saying's role is the conjectural first turn; the earth's report supplies the informed response. The inference is that the later report answers this precise question. The ayah consequently reads as the opening of an evidentiary exchange rather than only an uncontrolled cry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B011","root_001272/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c72f5978dee2c350a5b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:3:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B011\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İçteki varsayım ve belirsizlik, eylemler açığa çıkmadan önce soru biçiminde dışarı çıkar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8a06155c7fef6e4ce8c5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:3:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnsanın sorusu, olayı kavramaya çalışan ilk algının başarısızlığını gösterir; sonraki zorunlu gösterim ve doğrudan görme bu algıyı düzeltir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_000531/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8fbfae21932c793dc634","text":"1. **The human is a perceiver whose perception must be corrected.** Fixed `ء ن س/B002` supplies becoming aware by seeing, sensing, or hearing. The question `مَا لَهَا` follows the material disturbance, but 99:6 later uses causative showing (`ر ء ي/B012`) and 99:7-8 use direct seeing (`ر ء ي/B001`). The fixed human noun's role is therefore epistemic: it names the creature trying to bring an unfamiliar event into awareness. The inference is that the failed first perception produces the question and that the later display answers it. `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` thus reads less as a generic species label and more as a perceptual subject placed under correction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_926d1cbdb23198722a60","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:3:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Tekil insanın sözü, daha sonra dağılan kalabalığın ortak paniğini ve dolaşıma giren konuşmasını temsil ediyor olabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9430f3987cb1db0dc6dd","text":"3. **Question as circulating public talk.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B007` circulating talk/public report; activated by `ن و س B001` people’s swaying mass and `ش ت ت B001` scattered groups in 99:6. Structural cue: a singular `الإنسان` speaks, but later the mass of `الناس` issues in groups. Assigned role: the singular utterance condenses a collective public panic. Abductive move: I infer that the singular human can represent the aggregate whose talk will become many reports because the packet shifts from singular human to people in dispersion. Reading change: `وقال الإنسان` reads as the eruption of shared human discourse, not a lone individual’s sentence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_001272/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9befd7e2c31c2164bf19","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:3:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:3:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Görünür insan, konuşma ayrıcalığını yitirip aşağıdaki dünyanın tanıklığını dinleyen tarafa dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002","root_001272/B011","root_001272/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ccfa61d05be6530885a1","text":"4. **Inner saying exposed into speech.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B012` unspoken inner saying; `ق و ل B011` saying as supposing; activated by `ر ء ي B002` reflective judgment/opinion in 99:6-8 and `خ ب ر B001` inner knowledge in 99:4. Structural cue: the human’s `ما لها` precedes true news; it is a supposition/question before tested knowledge. Assigned role: speech reveals the interior uncertainty of the human. Abductive move: I supply that question-form discloses an inward judgment gap, because the packet later replaces supposition with seeing. Reading change: the ayah reads as exposure of human interiority before exposure of deeds.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e63b421af15f7221a10e","text":"99:4-5 supplied an informed, commissioned answer to the human's conjectural question, turning the apparent monologue into an exchange. More surprisingly, 99:6-8 shift from the human's attempted awareness (`ء ن س/B002`) to being made to see and then directly seeing (`ر ء ي/B012`, `B001`). The repeated singular conditionals in 99:7-8 further correct a species-level reading: the perceptual correction reaches each acting person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f25c735927b28b773d3d","text":"2. **The human as visible presence confronted by a speaking lower world.** Lexical evidence: `ء ن س B001` visible human presence over against hidden/wild beings; activated by `ء ر ض B001` lower ground in 99:1-2 and `ح د ث B003` earth’s speech/report in 99:4. Structural cue: the named human stands between earth’s output and earth’s report. Assigned role: the human is the visible conscious party who assumed the ground was mute, now facing an interlocutor from below. Abductive move: I supply that “earth speaks” reverses the normal human/ground hierarchy: the visible human becomes listener/questioner. Reading change: the ayah reads as social reversal: humanity loses exclusive control of speech.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f6fd19f41590cb6fc1fd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:3:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsanın en tanıdık maddi zemini, sarsıntıyla yabancılaşıp hakkında soru sorulan bir şeye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
